After serving the Vedic statements, the transcendental sound vibration, all other forms of evidence, such as hypothesis and direct perception, which are opposed to gaining understanding of the Vedas, also stops.

**Vedic statements are not accepted by those with unenlightened viewpoints**

If we process the transcendental sound vibration we hear from the spiritual master through an unenlightened viewpoint, then we will attribute fault to the transcendental sound vibration or to the Absolute Truth; we are bound to make a distinction between the sound and its object. But there is no such distinction whatsoever in the Supreme Lord, who is one without a second, because He is the supreme Absolute Truth. If we attribute imperfection to the Absolute Truth, we will be forced to eat our own words. There is no difference between transcendental sound and the intended object of that sound.

On the pretext of accepting and distorting the meaning of transcendental sound, the aversion to the worship of the Lord’s Saccidananda Deity form develops

In this world, one can become an enlightened spiritual expert by the process of aural reception. Some time ago, there appeared a flow of thought in the mind of an atheist and the anti-Hindu community. Their opinion was that if all ignorance can be eradicated by transcendental sound, why worship the Deity? According to them, worshiping the Lord’s Deity form is contrary to Vedic injunctions. They say that the Vaisnava practice of Deity worship is an imitation of Buddhist philosophyand not authorized by Vedic philosophy. As a proxy or replica of the forms we find in the spiritual world, which one cannot realize through one’s mundane senses, different Deities are created from stone, wood, or in paint.

**The Supreme Lord and His Deity form are nondifferent**

The holy name is Himself the Supreme Lord. To consider the forms, qualities, and pastimes of the Supreme Lord as different from the Lord is a misconception and opposed to advaya-jnana. My spiritual master instructed me not to consider the Lord’s Deity form a product of matter, or an object of sense gratification. Because we don t understand the actual meaning of the mantra we have been given, because we don’t understand the concept of oneness as it applies to the spiritual realm, we think there is a distinction between the worshipable Godhead and His Deity form. As soon as we see oneness between the Deity form and the Lord Himself, there is no such misconception or illusion.

**The spiritual master reveals the meaning of the mantra**

The spiritual master establishes the disciples’ connection with the Supreme Lord by teaching them the process of devotional service and giving them the responsibility to serve the Lord. The spiritual master reveals the purport of the mantra he gives to qualified candidates, but not to unqualified persons. The spiritual master should reveal the meaning of the mantra after the disciples undergo prescribed purificatory rituals. The Narada Pancaratra (Bharadvaja Samhita, chapter 2, text 34) states:

*svayam brahmani niksiptan jatan eva hi mantratah*  
*vinitan-artha putradin samskrtya prati-bodhayet*

An acarya should personally award Pancaratrika mantras, and by the strength of such mantras, the disciple becomes twiceborn. After purifying his surrendered disciples and sons by giving them 10, 16, 44, or 48 Vedic samskaras, an acarya should teach mantras to his celibate followers. This is the process of diksa, or initiation.

**Deity worship is something all together different from the idolatry practiced by sahajiyas and Buddhists**

Idolatry is very bad. A wooden lion sits still. As long as one considers the Deity to be made of wood or clay, one will fail to realize the truth. Sahajiyas and Buddhists worship a deity. This is why people like Sri Hara Prasada Sastn say that VaiSriavisim is either a branch of Buddhism or sahajiyism. When they talk about Vaisnavism, they accept the ideal of mundane sahajiyism as the ideal for Vaisnavas, and the so-called dharma of mundane sahajiyism as Vaisnava dharma.

**The difference between the idolatry practiced by atheistic Hindus who worship five gods and the worship of the Supreme Lord performed by Vaisnavas**

Buddhist philosophy has destroyed the authorized Vedic ideas. The deity worship of so-called Hindus and pantheists that we see these days is simply idol worship. The Vaisnavas never engage in idol worship. We never worship anyone but the supreme Absolute Truth, the Personality of Godhead. Vyasadeva, who is a representative of the disciplic succession, offers this evidence:

*arcye visnau sila-dhir gurusu nara-matir vaisnave jati-buddhir*  
*visnor va vaisnavanam kali-mala-mathane pada-tirthe ’mbu-buddhih*  
*sri-visnor namni mantre sakala-kalusa-he sabda-samanya-buddhir*  
*visnau sarvesvarese tad-itara-sama-dhir yasya va naraki sah*

**“No one should consider the Deity in the temple to be made of stone or wood, nor should one consider the spiritual master an ordinary human being. No one should consider a Vaisnava to belong to a particular caste or creed, and no one should consider caranamrta or Ganges water to be like ordinary water. Nor should anyone consider the Hare Krsna maha-mantra to be a material vibration. All these expansions of Krsna in the material world are simply demonstrations of the Lord’s mercy and willingness to give facility to His devotees who are engaged in His devotional service within the material world.”**

**The difference between idolaters and Vaisnavas**

The idolaters are degraded, because they consider the Deity to be made of stone. They think that the salagrama-sila is a stone from the Gandaki River. Hellish idolators think that the spiritual master is an ordinary human being who belongs to a particular caste. However, Vaisnavas do not encourage or engage in that type of idol worship. They do not consider the worshipable Deity form to be made of stone. They do not start their worship without hist performing the bhuta-suddhi, or meditation purifying their consciousness and very existence. They do not worship the Supreme Lord with the same mundane senses with which they access external sense objects, such as mundane form and taste.

**See Lord Visnu within each demigod; idolatry begins when one lacks knowledge of the five truths**

Take, for example, any demigod. The Vaisnavas see the Absolute Truth Lord Visnu as the Supersoul in each of the demigods. The sun rises in the sky. Within the sun globe is the sun god. Within the sun god is Baladeva Prabhu, in whose heart resides Mahalaksmi. Within the heart of Mahalaksmi lives Radha-Govinda, the Divine Couple engaged in spiritual pastimes. Our Baladeva Prabhu is subordinate to Radha-Govinda. We see demigod forms, but we fail to see Baladeva-Krsna within them. Because we do not perform bhuta-suddhi, we are unable to understand the five truths. If we fail to understand this, our worship will simply become idol worship.

**Only mundane sahajiyas make a distinction between the Supreme Lord’s body and the Lord Himself**

The Supreme Personality of Godhead Sri Caitanya is saccidananda and is manifest here in the Sri Saccidananda Matha. If we abandon the disciplic succession and adopt other paths, we will become idolaters, mundane sahajiyas, subscribers to unenlightened views, Mayavadis, or mundane philanthropists.

**The philosophy of Sri Caitanya**

Sri Caitanyacandra displayed the pastime of seeing Lord Jagannatha as the son of the king of Vraja. “The Supreme Lord is present within the idol made of neern wood” - Mahaprabhu did not exhibit such a misconception by discriminating between the Lord’s body and the Lord Himself, the way idolaters do. He said elsewhere, pratima naha tumi saksat brajendra nandana: “My dear Lord, You are not a statue; You are directly the son of Maharaja Nanda.”

**Internal vision**

The mood that arises in the heart driven by an unenlightened viewpoint distorts reality. When one is too much eager to know what has been written on a letter inside an envelope, one wastes no time paying attention to the envelope. When we focus our attention on the moon when seeing it through the branches of a tree, we feel no need to look at the branches themselves in order to enjoy the beauty of the moon. The indwelling Supersoul is not served by the processes of this external world. As scripture \[Bhakti-rasamrta-sindhu 1.2.234\] states:

*sevonmukhe hi jihvadau svayam eva sphuraty adah*

Worship of the Supreme Lord through the chanting of mantras or transcendental sound is different from the system practiced by the Buddhists. We should call out the names of the Supreme Lord in such a way that we automatically remember everything about Lord Caitanyacandra. We should worship the Supreme Lord with our mantras and not be misguided by the methods of worship practiced by Buddhists. We have to remain ever careful so that we become free from the ideas that lead us into hellish life.

**Vaisnava dharma is Vedic dharma**

The Supreme Lord is far away from the vision of the idolaters, but He is very near to the spiritual vision of devotees. Vaisnava dharma alone is the authorized Vedic dharma. Only the Vaisnavas can understand the actual purport of the Vedas.

**The gradual state of bhajana**

It is stated in Bhakti-rasamrta-sindhu (parva vibhaga, IV lahari, verses 15-16):

*adau sraddha tatah sadhu-sahgo ’tha bhajana-kriya*  
*tato ’nartha-nivrttih syat tato nistha rucis tatah*  
*athasaktis tato bhavas tatah premabhyudancati*  
*sadhakanam ayam premnah pradurbhave bhavet kramah*

**“In the beginning there must be faith. Then one becomes interested in associating with pure devotees. Thereafter one is initiated by the spiritual master and executes the regulative principles under his orders. Thus one is freed from all unwanted habits and becomes firmly fixed in devotional service. Thereafter, one develops taste and attachment. Tins is the way of sadhana-bhakti, the execution of devotional service according to the regulative principles. Gradually emotions intensify, and finally there is an awakening of love. This is the gradual development of love of Godhead for the devotee interested in Krsna consciousness.”**

**The method of worshiping the Lord practiced by liberated souls**

Liberated souls worship the Lord by chanting kirtana, mantras, and through other such processes. The transcendental sound vibration is nondifferent from the object it is naming. By following the gradual path, beginning from sraddha, we can develop love and devotion for the Supreme Lord in our heart.

**Conclusion**

In the Brahma-samhita (5.38) we find the following verse:

*premanjana-cchurita-bhakti-vilocanena*  
*santah sadaiva hrdayesu vilokayanti*  
*yam syamasundaram acintya-guna-svarupam*  
*govindam adi-purusam tarn aharh bhajami*

**“I worship Govinda, the primeval Lord, who is Syamasundara, Krsna Himself with inconceivable innumerable attributes, whom the pure devotees see in their heart of hearts with the eye of devotion tinged with the salve of love.”**

## Discourse 2

**Nonduplicitous Practice and Instructor**

Place: Sri Saccidananda Matha, Cuttack  
Time: Ekadasi, July 10, 1927

Sri Rupa’s glorification of Sri Krsna Caitanya

*namo maha-vadanyaya krsna-prema-pradaya te*  
*krsnaya krsna-caitanya-namne gaura-tvise namah*

One day, Sri Rupa Gosvami Prabhu offered his obeisances at the lotus feet of Sri Gaurasundara by chanting this verse at the Dasasvamedha Ghata in Prayaga.

**“O Krsna! I offer my obeisances to you. Your name is Sri Krsna Caitanya, Your complexion is golden, Your quality is magnanimity, and Your pastime is to distribute love of Krsna. To that Krsna I offer my obeisances.”**

Who is the listener here? Sri Gaurasundara. And who the speaker? Sri Rupa Gosvami.

**Exposing the futility of making a show of hearing; or a proud hearer making a pretext of humility**

A third proud person like me heard this. Who is free from false pride? Sri Caitanyadeva. Who is He? Let me speak about Him.

Sri Gaurahari teaches us to become humbler than a blade of grass.

Sri Gaurahari says:

*trnad. api sunicena taror api sahisnuna*  
*amanina manadena kirtaniyah sada harih*

When someone approaches Mahaprabhu and says, “You are the son of the king of Vraja,” He immediately blocks His ears and replies, “You should call Krsna, Krsna. I am an insignificant living entity. You should not call Me Krsna, God. ”

Who can perform hari-kirtana? One who is endowed with these four qualities can chant the holy name of Hari constantly: One should consider himself lower than grass in the street. Grass or straw is trampled by the feet of cows, asses, and human beings. If all the proud people of the world would sincerely think themselves lower than straw in the street, they would be able to constantly chant the holy name of Krsna.

**Who is most fortunate?**

Only one who chants the holy name of Krsna is most fortunate. This is stated in Srimad-Bhagavatam (2.1.11):

*etan nirvidyamananam icchatam akuto-bhayam*  
*yoginam nrpa nirnitam harer namanukirtanam*

**“O King, constant chanting of the holy name of the Lord after the ways of the great authorities is the doubtless and fearless way of success for all, including those who are free from all material desires, those who are desirous of all material enjoyment, and also those who are self-satisfied by dint of transcendental knowledge”.**

**The other qualities of a chanter of the holy name**

The second quality of one who chants the holy name is that he is supremely tolerant. The third quality is that he never demands or expects any respect for himself. A chanter must be free of false pride. He never claims proprietorship of anything material. His fourth quality is that he always gives respect to others.

**The mystery of Sri Gaurasundara’s ideal**

Sri Gaurasundara, who set an example of utmost humility and who taught complete humility, heard the following verse from the month of Rupa:

*namo maha-vadanyaya Krsna-prema-pradaya te*  
*krsnaya krsna-caitanya-namne gaura-tvise namah*

Love of Krsna, the fifth objective of life, disdains the four objectives of life, namely religiosity, economic development, sense gratification, and liberation. These are the things most people consider the ultimate goal of life. Those who take to krsna-prema can disregard the four material objectives. You, Sri Caitanya Mahaprabhu, are the distributor of that krsna-prema. You are Krsna. And even though You are Krsna, You are the bestower of love of Krsna. You are known as Krsna Caitanya, Gauranga. You are the most magnanimous. The same Gaurasundara who instructs everyone not to expect respect for oneself and to give all respect to others is now hearing Himself glorified by Rupa Gosvaml. How is that possible?

**The envious, proud person’s misconception about the nonduplicitous, exalted personality, the personification of trnad api sunicena**

In today’s meeting, an abominable and wretched person has taken charge of delivering a lecture.

*jagai madhai haite muni se papistha*  
*purisera krta haite muni se laghistha*  
*mora nama sune yei tara punya ksaya*  
*mora nama laya yei tara papa haya*

**“I am more sinful than Jagai and Madhai and even lower than the worms in the stool. Anyone who hears my name loses the results of his pious activities. Anyone who utters my name becomes sinful.”**

Persons from noble families, experienced persons, and great persons are naturally endowed with good qualities; but who can become such an animal as to be willing to sit and hear his own glories from distinguished persons? Only an extremely sinful person will do so. We have taken charge of accepting such a complaint. Other people are seated in ordinary sitting places, but I have been provided with a raised platform, as if to inform everyone “Look at this big animal from the zoo garden! How puffed up he is! Have you ever seen such a sinful and foolish big animal! On top of that, he is wearing a flower garland around his neck! How shamelessly he sits and hears his own praise with selected words and adjectives. He is happy, for he is acting against the teachings of Mahaprabhu. So how can such a foolish animal be delivered from his false ego and animal propensity?”

*amara ajnaya guru hana tarn’ ei desa*

I am a totally foolish person. Because I am very proud, nobody gives me good instruction. When no one instructed me, then I informed Mahaprabhu about it. I thought it is better that I give him the responsibility for me and see what He asks me to do. At that time, Sri Gaurasundara ordered me:

*yare dekha, tare kaha ‘krsna’-upadesa*  
*amara ajnaya guru hana tara’ ei desa*

**“Instruct everyone to follow the orders of Lord Sri Krsna as they are given in the Bhagavad-gita and Srimad-Bhagavatam. In this way become a spiritual master and try to liberate everyone in this land.”**

*kabhu na badhibe tomara visaya-taranga*  
*punarapi ei thani pabe mora sanga*

He who teaches that one should become lower than a blade of grass Himself says, amara ajnaya guru hana tara’ ei desa. Mahaprabhu is giving the order: “Act as a spiritual master, like Me. Whomever you meet, you instruct about this.” This is the order of Caitanyadeva. “Become a spiritual master on My order and deliver the people of the world. Deliver people from their ignorance.”

*na badhibe tomara visaya-tarahga - pabe mora sanga*

Whoever is hearing this is saying with folded hands, “I am an atheist and fallen. How can I become a spiritual master? You are the Supreme Personality of Godhead. You are the spiritual master of the universe. You can become a spiritual master, but I cannot.”

In reply, Mahaprabhu says:

*kabhu na badhibe tomara visaya-tarahga*  
*punarapi ei thani pabe mora sanga*

**“Sri Caitanya Mahaprabhu further advised the brahmana Kurina, ‘If you follow this instruction, your materialistic life at home will not obstruct your spiritual advancement. Indeed, if you follow these regulative principles, we will again meet here, or, rather, you will never lose My company.’”**

**In the Absolute Truth there is no fault from any angle of vision**

In this case, there is no opportunity to forget Krsna. An angle is produced if two lines meet at less than 180 or 360 degrees. On a plane \[flat surface\], however, which covers 360 degrees, there are no angles. If the Supreme Lord Absolute Truth is described by comparing Him to 360 degrees, then there can be no abomination or fault in Him from any angle.

**It is not desirable to become a sense enjoyer**

Sense objects cause us trouble. Form, taste, smell, touch, and sound agitate us in waves. This is why one should not become a sense gratifier. Sri Caitanyadeva said (as recorded in the Caitanya-candrodaya-nataka 8.23):

*niskincanasya bhagavad-bhajanonmukhasya*  
*param param jigamisor bhava-sagarasya*  
*sandarsanam visayinam atha yositam ca*  
*ha hanta hanta visa-bhaksanato ’py asadhu*

**“Alas, for a person who is seriously desiring to cross the material ocean and engage in the transcendental loving service of the Lord without material motives, seeing a materialist engaged in sense gratification or seeing a woman who is similarly interested is more abominable than drinking poison willingly.”**

One who desires to worship the Supreme Lord does not associate with materialistic people. The imperfect sights of the external world cover our God consciousness. As soon as our sense desires are met, we immediately forget the Supreme Lord and think of His devotees as inferior to us. One who desires to make advancement on the path of devotional service to the Supreme Lord should not look at the faces of materialists; nor should one mingle with those who are materially inclined. A materialist is defined as one who considers himself the enjoyer of sense objects. One should neither associate with women nor with those who associate with women. Our supreme physician, Lord Gaurasundara, instructed us not to associate with women.

A person engrossed in sense gratification is not fit to be called “guru”.

Mahaprabhu said \[Sri Caitanya-caritamrta, Madhya 7.128 and Adi 9.41\]:

*amara ajnaya guru hana tara’ ei desa*  
*bharata-bhumite haila manusya-janma yara*  
*janma sarthaka kari ’ kara para-upakara*

**“Give up envy and show compassion to all living entities. Do not take up the profession of spiritual master simply to become envious of others. Do not act as a spiritual master just to drown yourself in sense gratification. But if you can become My sincere servant, and if you have received My mercy and empowerment, you have nothing to fear. “**

**A sincere follower of the disciplic succession has nothing to fear**

I have no fear. “My spiritual master heard this from his spiritual master.” Therefore, he accepted even an atheist like me and ordered me, *amara ajnaya guru hana tara’ei desa.*

To judge that the spiritual master who teaches his disciple to worship the spiritual master in disciplic succession lacks humility is mundane sahajiyism

Those who have not heard Gaurasundara’s statement must be thinking, “Just see how He is hearing His own glories!” When the spiritual master teaches his disciple the Eleventh Canto of Srimad-Bhagavatam, can anyone imagine what kind of atheism he is encouraging? What will an acarya do while explaining the verse that begins, *acaryam mam vijaniyat*: “Never disregard the acarya. Never consider the acarya equal to you.”? Should an acarya run from his vyasasana while explaining these statements, which were made by Krsna and by which people will benefit? An acarya is duty bound to speak the truth, for he has been given that privilege by his spiritual master. If he does not follow his duty, then his fall down is inevitable - he will be committing the third offense against the chanting of the holy name, which is to disobey the orders of the spiritual master.

When the spiritual master awards mantra initiation to his disciples, shouldn’t he tell them to worship the spiritual master with the mantra? Or should he tell them instead to beat their spiritual master with shoes? “One should never become envious of the spiritual master, for the spiritual master is the embodiment of all the demigods.” Shouldn’t the spiritual master explain all these things while teaching Srimad-Bhagavatam to his disciples?

“Just as one should have transcendental devotion for Lord Krsna, so he should have transcendental devotion for his spiritual master. Confidential subject matters are revealed to a person who has developed such sincere devotional service.” Shouldn’t the spiritual master tell his disciples this? “First, one should worship the spiritual master. The spiritual master must be adored first. The spiritual master should be treated with the same devotion with which one treats Krsna. This is how one worships the spiritual master.” Should the spiritual master run away and not disclose these facts to his disciples?

Ignorant souls who have fallen into illusion cannot understand the all-auspicious service performed by liberated souls.

The angle presents a deficiency in the sense that it does not make 180 or 360 degrees, but on the plane \[of 180s degrees\] or on something that is 360 degrees, there is no such fault. Similarly, there is no fault in liberation. This ordinary foolish people cannot understand. \[Sri Gurvastakam (7) states:\]

*saksad-dharitvena samasta-sastrair*  
*uktas tatha bhavyata eva sadbhih*  
*kintu prabhor yah ptiya eva tasya*  
*vande guroh sri-caranaravindam*

**If one does not treat the spiritual master as nondifferent from and most dear to the Supreme Personality of Godhead, one becomes disqualified as a disciple and chanter of the holy name of Hari.**

As one treats the Supreme Lord, so he should treat the spiritual master. Never consider the spiritual master to be less than the Supreme Lord in any way. It is the duty of devotees, learned persons, and brahmanas who know the Vedas to understand, worship, and serve the spiritual master as they would God. If they fail to treat the spiritual master in this way, their position as disciples will lessen. Unless one accepts the living spiritual master as nondifferent from the Supreme Lord and as the manifestation of the Supreme Lord, one can never utter the Lord’s holy name. There is evidence of this in the Veda \[Svetasvatara Upanisad 6.23\]:

*yasya deve para bhaktir yatha deve tatha gurau*  
*tasyaite kathita hy arthah prakasante mahatmanah*

**The spiritual master is the servitor-god, the asraya-god**

*amara prabhura prabhu sri-gaurasundara*  
*(Sri Caitanya-bhagavata, Adi 7.153)*

*yadyapi amara guru—caitanyera dasa*  
*tathapi jatniye ami tanhara prakasa*

The saccidananda Supreme Lord has hands, and with His hands He scratches His leg. The Lord’s hands are His body - He serves Himself. In order to personally teach us how to serve Him, the Supreme Lord incarnates as the spiritual master. In that sense, my spiritual master is nondifferent from the Supreme Lord; he and the Supreme Lord are one body. The spiritual master is the servitor-god, and the Supreme Lord is the worshipable God; the spiritual master is the sheltered-god, and the Supreme Lord is the object of service.

Lord Mukunda is the worshipable God, and the spiritual master, who is dearest to Lord Mukunda, is the servitor-god. No one is dearer to God than my spiritual master. He alone is extremely dear to the Supreme Lord. Our spiritual master said:

*na dharmam nadharmam sruti-gana-niruktam kila kuru*  
*vraje radha-krsna-pracura-paricaryam ilia tanu*  
*saci-sunum nandisvara-pati-sutatve guru-varam*  
*mukunda-presthatve smara param ajasram nanu manah*

**“My dear mind! Please do not perform the religious activities prescribed in the Vedas, nor the irreligious works forbidden by these scriptures. The ultimate conclusion of the entire Vedic literature is that the Divine Couple, Sri Sri Radha Krsna, is the Supreme object of worship, and that loving service to Them is the most relishable experience of all. Therefore, O mind, engage yourself fully in serving Them with love and devotion. Always meditate upon Sri Caitanya Mahaprabhu, the son of Sri Saci-mata, with the understanding that He is absolutely nondifferent from Sri Krsna, the son of Maharaja Nanda. Dear mind, also meditate upon Sri Guru, who is a dearmost devotee of Sri Mukunda Krsna.” (Manah Siksa, verse 2)**

*gurau gosthe gosthalayisu sujane bhusurajane*  
*svamantre srinanmi braja-nava yuva dvandvasarane*  
*sada dambham hitva kuru ratim apurvam atitaram*  
*aye svantar bhratas catubhirabhiyace dhrta padah*

**“O mind, I grasp your feet and beg you with sweet words: Please cast away all hypocrisy and develop intense, unprecedented love for my spiritual master, Vrajabhumi, the people of Vraja, the Vaisnavas, the brahmanas, the Gayatri mantra, the holy name, and the transcendental shelter that is the fresh young couple of Vraja, Radha and Krsna.” (Manah Siksa, verse 1)**

**Duplicity is an impediment to perfection**

If we eat less than we want in the name of courtesy, we will remain hungry. If we cheat the blacksmith of steel, \[we will get a substandard implement.\] If we are too shy to tell the teacher that we cannot understand the mathematics class, we will not achieve perfection in our field.

**Duplicity is not humility**

As you have appeared on the stage, you should not be shy. I am acting as the spiritual master, but if I say, “Do not glorify me,” then turn it around and say, “Glorify me more,” I am speaking nothing but duplicity.

**This spiritual master and Gauranga do not teach duplicity**

Our spiritual master has never taught such duplicity. Mahaprabhu did not teach such duplicity. We should serve the Supreme Lord with utmost simplicity. The words of the Supreme Lord are preserved in my spiritual master, and I will sincerely follow them without duplicity.

**To simply follow the teachings of guru and Gauranga is service to Krsna**

I will not disobey the orders of the spiritual master by hearing statements by ignorant people and the envious. When Sri Gaurasundara ordered me, amara ajnaya guru hana tara’ ei desa, and when this order reached my spiritual master and my spiritual master ordered me in the same way, I will not take shelter of duplicity when following that order. I will not follow the example of the foolish, cheating, false people. I will not learn hypocrisy. Materialistic people, envious people, pseudo renunciants, and the selfish cannot understand how the devotees of the Supreme Lord sacrifice everything in this world and, by the Lord’s order, are never disconnected from sincere service to the Lord even for a moment.

**The illusion of cheaters who desire material fame**

Cheaters, so-called Vaisnavas, and those desiring material fame, think, “How this person hears his own praises from his disciples while he sits on the seat of the guru! ” Every Vaisnava considers every other Vaisnava superior to himself. When Haridasa Thakura displays his humble, submissive nature, Mahaprabhu says, “You are the best of all. You are the crest jewel of all the people in the world. Come, let us sit together and take a meal.” Sri Caitanya Mahaprabhu even carried the transcendental body of Thakura Haridasa on His own lap. The principle of amani and munada is prominent among Sri Rupa’s followers. Those who find fault in that behavior are offenders. They are just like day-blind owls.

**Instructions regarding the nonduplicitous behavior of a real spiritual master and a disciple who makes a pretext of humility**

However, such topics are not applicable to a foolish, proud, insignificant, abominable, sinful, dog-eater like me. But, I say, this is not about my proper code of conduct; it is the rule for the whole human society. This rule has reached me through the disciplic succession. If I disobey it, then the fault of disregarding the spiritual master’s order will come upon me and remove me from the lotus feet of my spiritual master.

To follow the order of the Vaisnava spiritual master even if I have to become proud, even if I have to become an animal, even if I have to go to hell forever - I want to go to such a hell forever on that contract. Disregarding the order of my spiritual master, I will not follow the words of envious people. Disobeying the order of spiritual master, I will not follow anyone else’s order in the whole world. I will smash with my fist the mentality of the people of the world with the strength of the lotus feet of my spiritual master -I am so proud. If a little pollen from the dust of the lotus feet of my spiritual master is spread around, then millions of people like you will be delivered. There is no learning in the world, there is no proper philosophy within the fourteen worlds, there is nothing in human beings or demigods that can weigh more than a particle of dust from the lotus feet of my spiritual master.

**The spiritual master is the most nonduplicitous, nonviolent, compassionate person**

The spiritual master is never envious of anyone. I am not at all prepared to listen to someone who is envious of me. I am not ready to accept such a person as my spiritual master. Sri Svarupa Damodara stood face to face with Sri Caitanyadeva and said this:

*heloddhunita-khedaya visadaya pronmilad-amodaya*  
*samyac-chastra-vivadaya rasa-daya cittarpitonmadaya*  
*sasvad-bhakti-vinodaya sa-madaya madhurya-maryadaya*  
*sri-caitanya daya-nidhe tava daya bhuyad amandodaya*

**“O ocean of mercy, Sri Caitanya Mahaprabhu! Let there be an awakening of Your auspicious mercy, which easily drives away all kinds of material lamentation by making everything pure and blissful. Indeed, Your mercy awakens transcendental bliss and covers all material pleasures. By Your auspicious mercy, quarrels and disagreements arising among different scriptures are vanquished. Your auspicious mercy pours forth transcendental mellows and thus causes the heart to jubilate. Your mercy, which is full of joy, always stimulates devotional service and glorifies conjugal love of God. May transcendental bliss be awakened within my heart by Your causeless mercy.” (Sri Caitanya-caritamrita, Madhya 10.119)**

**Sri Caitanyadeva’s pastime of not expecting respect for Himself is meant to teach ns fools genuine humility, not duplicity**

When Sri Svarupa Damodara spoke like this, Sri Caitanyadeva heard it. But sometimes, in order to teach humility to fools, the Lord said, “You should not speak about Me like that.” However, He did not say this in order to teach duplicity \[in the form of false humility\].

**Answer the doubts of envious, ignorant people**

Foolish people are naturally suspicious. To reply to their doubts, I have given only a partial reply. I have no right to interfere with so much of their time in a single day.

**Seeing the pastime of amani and manada displayed by the exalted spiritual master, to then actually consider him unqualified is a grave offense**

*I have learned from my spiritual master:*  
*jagai madhai haite muni se papistha*  
*purisera kita haite muni se laghistha*

**“I am more sinful than Jagai and Madhai and even lower than worms in stool.” (Sri Caitanya-caritamrta, Adi 5.205)**

It is true that I am lower than worms in stool, but when my spiritual master displays such behavior on the order of his spiritual master and Mahaprabhu, may no one offend his lotus feet.

**Humility and the conclusion**

All of you display compassion for me, because I am the most afflicted person. You are all very liberal. You have forgiven many people. Similarly, you favor me by showing such forgiveness to this most prideful person.

*vancha-kalpatarubhyas ca*  
*krpa-sindhubhya eva ca*  
*patitanam pavanebhyo*  
*vaisnavebhyo namo namah*

## Discourse 3

**Initiation and Spiritual Enlightenment**

Place: Sri Sanatana Gaudiya Matha, Kasi Dhama  
Time: Sunday Afternoon, October 2, 1927  
(During the installation of the Sri Gaurahga Deity)

Invocation by the exalted follower of Sri Rupa while offering obeisances to Sri Sanatana, the acarya of sambandha-jnana

*vairagya-yug-bhakti-rasam prayatnair*  
*apayayan mam anabhipsum andham*  
*krpambudhir yah para-duhkha-duhkhi*  
*sanatanas tam prabhum asrayami*

**“I was unwilling to drink the nectar of devotional service filled with renunciation, but Sanatana Gosvami, out of his causeless mercy, made me drink it, even though I was otherwise unable to do so. Therefore, he is an ocean of mercy. He is very compassionate to fallen souls like me, and thus it is my duty to offer my respectful obeisances to his lotus feet.” (Vilapa-kusumanjali, Verse Six)**

**Varanasi Ksetra**

Today we are present at Varanasi-ksetra. Here, Sri Gaurasundara taught sanatana-dharma to Sanatana Prabhu, the spiritual master of Rupa Gosvami Prabhu, our previous acarya. The above-mentioned verse was composed and included by Srila Dasa Gosvami Prabhu in glorification of Sanatana Prabhu in his book Vilapa Kusumanjali.

The acarya for sambandha-jnana strongly encourages even the hesitant to drink the mellows of devotional service.

I lack the power of discrimination and I don’t know what is right and wrong. I am maddened by what I have learned from my senses and thus have been busy performing a whimsical form of bhajana. Then that greatly magnanimous Srila Sanatana Prabhupada, the eternal servant of the greatly magnanimous Gaurasundara, forced me to drink the nectar of devotional service by removing my thirst for material desires. Our spiritual master is Rupa, and his spiritual master is Sanatana.

**Only those who follow Rupa are eligible to hear about the devotional service performed by the gopis**

Sanatana is the sambandha-jnanacarya. He has vividly described sambandha-jnana in his Sri Brhad-Bhagavatamrta. If we wish to take shelter of the Lord’s lotus feet, we have no alternative but to take shelter of the Lord’s devotees. Our predecessor acarya, Srila Rupa Gosvami, whom Raghunatha dasa Gosvami accepted as his master and from whom our Kaviraja Gosvami attained the science of bhajana, enacted the pastime of hearing about the devotional service practiced by the gopis - a bhakti rare even for Uddhava and others - from Sri Sanatana Gosvami Prabhu.

**The conditioning of perpetually adverse living entities and how to help them**

As a result of the deeds we have been performing since time immemorial, we have concluded that simply to accumulate the sensations of material enjoyment without any relation to Krsna is our ultimate goal of life. We are fallen souls. We cannot bring about our own auspiciousness. Unless and until we take shelter at the lotus feet of the deliverer of the fallen soul, we cannot achieve any benefit. The Six Gosvamis, headed by Rupa and Sanatana, are eternal associates of the Supreme Lord. They are the spiritual masters of all living entities. Their lotus feet are the highest shelter for all beings.

**“Yatanavata” and Srila Sanatana’s history**

Mahaprabhu was living at the house of Candrasekhara in a place near Yatanavata, in Kasi. A Maharashtrian brahmana and Tapana Misra, who was the father of Raghunatha Bhatta Gosvami, were engaged in His service there. Prior to this, Rupa Gosvami Prabhu enacted the pastime of renouncing his house. Sanatana deceived the prison guard with a bribe and rushed to the lotus feet of Mahaprabhu. Sanatana then enacted the pastime of being captured by the dacoits near Muzzafarpur. At that time, Srila Sanatana was known as Sakara Mallika. When Sanatana arrived in Kasi, Tapana Misra, seeing his beard and mustache, thought him a Muslim mendicant. Mahaprabhu then changed Sanatana’s name from Sakara Mallika to Sanatana. Instead of giving him a dress befitting a Karnataka brahmana, the Lord dressed him like a beggar. Sanatana dressed himself with the unwashed cloth remnants of a Vaisnava.

**Paramahamsa garb and vaidhi garb**

Used cloth is the proper dress for a niskincana, a Vaisnava paramahamsa on the path of raga-bhakti. It is not the dress for those on the path of vaidhi-bhakti. Saffron cloth is meant for the vaidhi-bhaktas.

**Sambandha-jnana**

Sriman Mahaprabhu taught Sanatana Gosvami about the jiva’s relationship with the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Where there is more than one, there is a relationship between them - the mood of worshipable and worshiper.

**Remembrance of Krsna**

Srimad-Bhagavatam (12.12.55) states:

*avismrtih krsna-padaravindayoh*  
*ksinoty abhadrani ca sam tanoti*  
*sattvasya suddhim paramatma-bhaktim*  
*jnanam ca vijnana-viraga-yuktam*

**“Remembrance of Lord Krsna’s lotus feet destroys everything inauspicious and awards the greatest good fortune. It purifies the heart and bestows devotion for the Supreme Soul, along with knowledge enriched with realization and renunciation.”**

**Sambandha-jnana is itself transcendental knowledge, or initiation**

First, there is sambandha-jnana, knowledge of one’s relationship with the Supreme Lord. Another name for sambandha-jnana is “transcendental knowledge” or “initiation.” This transcendental knowledge is obtained by hearing from the lotus mouth of the spiritual master. It is said in Sri *Hari-bhakti-vilasa* (2.3—1):

*divyam jnanam yato dadyat kuryat papasya sanksayam*  
*tasmat dikseti sa prokta desikais tattva-kovidaih*

**“Diksa is the process by which one can awaken his transcendental knowledge and vanquish all reactions caused by sinful activity. A person expert in the study of the revealed scriptures knows this process as diksa.”**

**The real meaning of diksa, or initiation**

Initiation does not simply mean the acceptance of mantras. Initiation refers to that function by which one attains transcendental knowledge. The living entities cannot bring about their own benefit by reading hundreds of books and making a show of performing some sort of whimsical bhajana. The meaning of the mantras helps the meaning of the sound vibration. That a disciple is simply receiving transcendental knowledge from a living spiritual master is based on ideas from an unenlightened viewpoint if it is according to the definition of initiation in the external world. However, this is not the case with the genuine disciple. Rather, at initiation the disciple’s heart is captured by spiritual enlightenment, or the actual meaning of the mantra.

**A Vaisnava gives up bad association**

Sri Gaurasundara said:

*niskincanasya bhagavad-bhajanonmukhasya*  
*param param jigamisor bhava-sagarasya*  
*sandarsanam visayinam atha yositam ca*  
*ha hanta hanta visa-bhaksanato ’py asadhu*

**“Greatly lamenting, the Lord then informed Sarvabhauma Bhattacarya, ‘Alas, for a person who is seriously desiring to cross the material ocean and engage in the transcendental loving service of the Lord without material motives, seeing a materialist engaged in sense gratification or seeing a woman who is similarly interested is more abominable than drinking poison willingly.’” (Sri Caitanya-caritamrta, Madhya 11.8)**

**The teachings of Sri Caitanya have not vanished into the material sky but are eternally present in the spiritual sky with fall enlightenment**

The historians may say that the teachings Caitanyadeva imparted many years ago have vanished into the sky. Although they appear to the unenlightened to have vanished into thin air, the words the Supreme Lord spoke are constantly vibrating in the ears of the service-inclined persons in the eternal spiritual sky, Vaikuntha. That is the enlightened viewpoint.

**A Vaisnava is not an idol worshiper**

We do not worship ordinary idols. We are eager to worship the Supersoul according to our spiritual nature. Our spirit souls are engaged in the worship of the Supreme Lord, but at present, that natural mentality of a worshiper has changed.

**The duty of those who live in the abode of liberation**

The duty of those eager to achieve the abode of liberation is to discuss Gaurasundara’s teachings. We should not relegate Gaurasundara to some Vedic brahmana or religious preacher. Rather, we should know that He is directly the son of the king of Vraja. “We belong to Krsna’s marginal energy” - we should make all endeavors to revive this understanding in our spiritual consciousness. As Srimad-Bhagavatam (11.9.29) states:

*labdhva su-durlabham idam bahu-sambhavante*  
*manusyam artha-dam anityam apiha dhirah*  
*turnam yateta na pated anu-mrtyu yavan*  
*nihsreyasaya visayah khalu sarvatah syat*

**“After many, many births and deaths one achieves the rare human form of life, which, although temporary, affords one the opportunity to attain the highest perfection. Thus a sober human being should quickly endeavor for the ultimate perfection of life as long as his body, which is always subject to death, has not fallen down and died. After all, sense gratification is available even in the most abominable species of life, whereas Krsna consciousness is possible only for a human being.”**

**What is the highest or only real goal of human life?**

We will get material enjoyment life after life. We can enjoy the fruits of our actions as enjoyers. We can enjoy the happiness of deep sleep and dreaming. But we should set aside our many future births for such enjoyments. In this life we must try our best to cultivate the most important task at hand - a task not possible to accomplish in any life but a human one.

Had we taken birth as demigods, we would not have become qualified to hear hari-katha; nor would we have had the opportunity. We should try our level best, therefore, in this human life, to perfect our self-interest. Religiosity, economic development, sense gratification, and liberation are not the actual goals of life. We cannot accomplish our real self-interest through any of these means. Having given up the insignificant desire for material enjoyment chased after by materialists, and the insignificant desire for godless liberation chased after by dry renunciants, those who value their own self-interest do not come to Varanasi, the abode of liberation. Rather, those who have heard about the ultimate goal of life hanker after Krsna’s service as practiced by Rudra while he resided in this liberation-awarding dhama.

Krsna-prema is not an insignificant attainment, like the achievement of losing one’s identity. Anyone and everyone can understand this if they think about it without bias. Sri Rudradeva always chants the holy name of Rama, and Ramacandra is also very fond of Rudra. If we fail to enter such pastimes, we will have to continue in the cycle of repeated birth and death. Unless we are engaged in the service of Sankarsana-Rama in submission to Rudra, we will continue in the cycle of birth and death. Rudra constantly teaches this with his five mouths.

**Eternal, sacred Kasi is a place for reviving knowledge of the living entities’ relationship with the Supreme Lord**

Paying attention to what Caitanyadeva says in reply to Sanatana Gosvami Prabhu’s questions will check our desires for material enjoyment and liberation. Kasi-ksetra is not a place for sense gratification. It is a place for worshiping Lord Visnu and the Vaisnavas - a place for spiritual cultivation. If we consider this dhama, which can award liberation, a place to come and enjoy ourselves, then no one will praise our intelligence. Here, the eternal Gaurasundara is teaching the eternal Vaisnava Sanatana Gosvami about Lord Hari. This place stimulates the living entities’ relationship with the Supreme Lord. This is why we take shelter of the lotus feet of Mahadeva, who is most-dear to Lord Hari.

**The Deity form is not a product of matter; the murti is directly the Supreme Lord**

Please do not consider the Lord’s Deity to be made of wood, stone, or other forms of matter. Just as Mahaprablm taught Sanatana Gosvami Prabhu about Lord Hari, so Sri Gaurasundara is speaking hari-katha at this Sri Sanatana Gaudiya Matha in His transcendental Deity form. All of you please come and take darsana of Sri Gaurasundara.

## Discourse 4

## Discourse 5

**Address on Sri Vyasa-puja Day**

**Invocation in glorification of Vyasa**

We recite this verse from the invocation to the *Mahabharata:*

Today also, at the commencement of the new year, we glorify Sri Krsna Dvaipayana Vyasa. Lord Narayana is the worshipable Lord of Srimad Vyasa and the Supreme Personality of Godhead. His internal potency is Sarasvatidevi, or Narayana. After offering respectful obeisances unto this Couple, one glorifies the spiritual master, Sri Vyasa. This is the eternal and traditional custom.

**The original ingredient and the spiritual master of the material world**

The human form of the Supreme Lord, who is the grandfather of all living entities, and His replica, the four-headed Brahma, are the original ingredients of the material world.

**The statement of jagad-guru Sridhara Svami**

Sridhara Svami, a descendant of Sri Visnuswami, said:

**The original living spiritual master, the four-headed Brahma , accepted Lord Nrsimhadeva's** ***samvit*** **potency into his heart, and goddess Sarasvati sang the four Vedas through Brahma's four mouths. Narada heard those Vedas exactly. When Narada spoke the same knowledge as it is, Sri Vyasadeva heard it and captured it in** ***his*** **heart.**

For the benefit of the members of the disciplic succession, Vyasa spoke about the favorable and unfavorable processes of cultivating devotion at the lotus feet of the Supreme Lord Narayana. He promoted the infallible among them and removed all processes that were fallible.

**The pastime of Sri Nrisimha**

The pastime of that Lord Visnu who imparted spiritual enlightenment to the heart of Brahma is called Sri Nrisimha lila*.* This is why Sridhara, who is a descendant of Visnu Svamipada, while ascertaining the most essential form of his worshipable Lord Narayana, arranged the *darsana* of Sri Nrisimhadeva and thereby revealed the infallible form of the Lord for his followers.

**Sri Vyasa-puja, following in the footsteps of Sri Nityananda**

Today, following in the footsteps of Sri Nityananda, we are preparing to offer *arghya* to worship Sri Vyasadeva. One day in Navadvipa, *jagad-guru* Sri Nityananda Prabhu personally performed this offering of *arghya* in worship of the original spiritual master, Brahma down to his disciple Narada and to his disciple Vedavyasa to his disciple Srimadananda Tirtha to Sri Nityananda, Sri Advaitacarya, and the entire Gaudiya Vaisnava society. Following the custom of the disciplic succession, conducting such a ceremony is a principal function in our execution of devotional service.

**To repeat the words of the Vedas spoken by Sri Vyasa is the way the acaryas worship Sri Vyasa**

The  *acaryas* simply repeat the Vedic statements spoken by Sri Vyasa. Following in his footsteps, we have also prepared to offer homage at the lotus feet of the spiritual master.

**The speciality of the fifth day of the waxing and waning moons in the month of Magha**

In order to please the goddess of material learning, children offer flowers on the fifth day of the waxing moon in the month of Magha. We are offering flowers at the lotus feet of the goddess of spiritual learning on the fifth day of the waning moon in the month of Magha. This offering is coming directly from the lotus hands of Sri Vyasadeva.

**The invocation mantra for Sri Vyasa-puja**

*yasya deve par bhaktir yath deve that gurau*  
*tasyaite kathit hy artha parkasante mahatmanah*

In order to attain the qualification to hear this mantra, we arrange to perform the Sri Vyasa-puja sacrifice.

**Sriman Madhvacarya’s ideal example as an acarya**

One day, Srimadananda Tirthapada, while engaged in the worship of Sri Vyasa, acted as an  *acarya* by sitting on the *vyasasana.* Following in his footsteps, his subordinates have been sitting on *vyasasanas* ever since in order to explain *Srimad-Bhagavatam.* It is true that one feels hesitant to sit on the *vyasasana,* considering himself unqualified by material qualification, but may the sinful tendency to transgress the spiritual master’s order never turn us away from serving the lotus feet of the spiritual master. This is our humble request at the lotus feet of Sri Vyasadeva and Sripada Sriman Madhvacarya.

Sri Madhvamuni enacted the pastime of acting as an  *acarya* for eighty years in order to teach us how to worship Sri Vyasadeva. One time, he appeared as Bhima and served Krsna during the *Mahabharata* battle simply to show the godless people how to serve the Lord who carries a club in His hand. Another time, he appeared as Hanum n and helped Sri Ramacandra in His mission by destroying the unfavorable elements. Again it is he who eternally sustains the Vaikuntha planet in the form of Vayu and thus keeps his transcendental, eternal existence intact.

Srimadananda Tirtha disappeared on the ninth day of the waxing moon, and today, his descendants - our spiritual teachers - have appointed me as the priest for Sri Vyasa-puja. This is just a pastime in order to observe proper etiquette in serving the *maha-bhagavata.* As per my constitutional identity, I know that I am an unqualified, unspeakable, eternal servant of the followers of Sri Rupa. Therefore, it is simply because of my disqualification that I show my current restlessness. If my listeners can forgive my audacity, then I will become eligible to serve them. If you press my throat, then I will not be allowed to worship Sri Vyasa. Therefore, my heartiest prayer to all of you is that since you have given the right to countless great personalities to worship Sri Vyasa, you kindly also give me the right to offer such worship, if only for a moment.

**Following the mood of the disciplic succession, the priest for the Sri Vyasa-puja ceremony glorifies Lord Hari with unhypocritical humility and special tolerance**

As the priest for Sri Vyasa-puja , I am climbing on the *vyasasana* with great difficulty. The worship I am offering for the pleasure of my spiritual master and his predecessors is established according to the descending path. I do not want to be deceived by the hope of attaining the unconquerable object through mundane mental speculation. That is why being faithful to the orders of the descendants of Brahma , to the orders of the followers of Sri Vyasa, and to the unlimited mercy of the followers of Rupa, today I offer my homage at Sri Vyasa's feet with a brief description of the path of devotional service as spoken by Rupa.

I am not only offering homage to the lotus feet of Sri Vyasa, but in order to offer my homage to the lotus feet of the Gaudiya  *acaryas* who are disciples of Sri Vyasa, on the order of my spiritual master I am audacious enough to give up the disgusting mentality adopted by those who follow the ascending path. In my act, I do not neglect the principle of *trnad api sunicena.*

While speaking the glories of Sri Rupa's followers, my mood of being as tolerant as a tree is not disturbed. Despite a sinful desire for material fame, I am not violating my spiritual master’s words. Rather, with the desire to be initiated into the principle of giving others respect, as taught by Sri Guru and Gauranga, I repeat the transcendental subject matters by giving up the mentality of judging others. I am not entitled to the good and bad results of this ceremony. Therefore, I am neither a candidate for worldly praise nor criticism. Rupa, the eternal associate of the Supreme Lord, his followers, and all his future followers are my worshipable spiritual masters. As such they are under the shelter of Brahma, Narada, Vyasa, Madhva, and Sri Nityananda Prabhu. My life will be successful if I can take eternal shelter of these personalities. Then my material disease will be vanquished and I will consider the two witches (material enjoyment and liberation) to be Putanas rather than my mothers. May I become a worthy son of my mother Bhaktidevi, whose sons are knowledge and renunciation, and not be indifferent in her service. May I not think of Mother Bhakti as my maidservant. This is my appeal.

**Sri Sanatana, Sri Rupa, Sri Raghunatha, and Sri Jiva are the victory flag of Sri Caitanya’s teachings**

Sri Caitanyadeva trained Rupa Goswami Prabhu in the science of devotional service and taught *sambandha-jnana* to Sri Sanatana Prabhu by making him the  *acarya* of the devotional conclusion. Those same teachings are reflected in Jiva, and shine brightly in him as the  *acarya* of *Srimad-Bhagavatam.* We find the devotional form of a personified disciple of Sri Rupa beautifully in Raghunatha Dasa Prabhu, who was a disciple of Svarupa. These teachings incessantly fill the lakelike hearts of the Gaudiya Vaisnavas with the water of hope. On the banks of the lakelike heart of Rupa's Jiva lies the protector of the nectarean ocean of the mellows of devotional service; Jiva resolves everything that opposes the *siddhanta* and the staunches the flow of the muddy water of *anarthas.*

**Srila Jiva Goswami does not preach against the paṣcaratra system or any conclusion opposed to Rupa**

Sri Rupa Goswami illumined devotion to Sri Caitanya with his own lotus hands. What Sri Jiva taught in his *Durgama-sangamani* commentary does not dismantle the *Pascaratra* system. Followers of Rupa-Raghunatha do not agree with those who think that, in order to oppose *paṣcaratrika,* Jiva more or less discouraged people from following Rupa and instead established the importance of seminal succession and *svakhya-vada.* These considerations are abominable. Gaudiya Vaisnavas like Sriman Krsnadasa Kaviraja Goswami and Sriman Narottama Prabhu are the physicians for the deadly disease of bad faith. Texts like the *Manah Siksa* and *Vil pa Kusumanjali* are the prime essence of what it means to follow Rupa.

Sitting on the *vyasasana* after having surrendered to Jiva, may we identify ourselves as pure *jivas.* May we sit on the *vyasasana* surrendered to the teachings of Svarupa's Raghunatha. May our transcendental service inclination be awakened and our mundane restlessness removed so that we can smoothly perform Vyasa-puja . May the water of our servitorship to the feet of Svarupa's Raghu, fetched in the pitcher of Jiva's teachings, which are based on *Sri* *Bhakti-rasamrta-sindhu,* protect us from the hands of the six plunderers who appear in the form of the temporary servants of our six enemies, headed by lust, and thus bathe us in and quench our thirst for the waters of immortality.

*gurau gosthe gosthalayisu sujane bhusurajane*  
*svamantre srinamni braja-nava yuva dvandvacarane*  
*sada dambham hitva kuru ratim apurvam atitara maye*  
*svantar bhratas catubhir abhiyace dhrta padah*

**“O mind, I grasp your feet and beg you with sweet words: Please cast away all hypocrisy and develop intense, unprecedented love for my spiritual master, Vrajabhumi, the people of Vraja, the Vaisnavas, the** ***brahmanas*****, the Gayatri mantra, the holy name, and the transcendental shelter that is the fresh young couple of Vraja, Radha and Krsna.” (*****Manah Siksa,*** **verse 1)**

**Sanatana, Rupa, Raghunatha, and Jiva are one; the illusion of some who think they are following Rupa**

When Rupa Goswami was hearing from Sri Caitanyadeva, he was addressed as *Dabir Khasa,* based on who he had been before meeting Sri Caitanyadeva. When he was enlightened in the science of devotional service, his followers knew him by the name Rupa, a name given to him by Sriman Mahaprabhu. Rupa enacted the pastime of receiving knowledge about devotional service from Sri Gaurasundara. Whatever he heard he presented as the *Bhakti-rasamrta-sindhu,* which has become, for his followers, the law book for devotional service. The same Sri Krsna, who is the object of *sambandha-tattva* and from whom Rupa received the devotional conclusion, awarded Rupa's brother Sakara Mallika with the name Sanatana. He did this in His form as Sri Krsna Caitanya. This is why Rupa glorifies Sanatana Prabhu as his spiritual master. It is also why Raghunatha Dasa Goswami Prabhu named Sanatana as the compiler of *Krsna-sambandha* in his book the *Vilapa Kusumanjali.*

Krsnadasa Kaviraja Goswami Prabhu called Sanatana the *bhakti-siddhanta acarya.* Decorated by the lotus hands of Sri Rupa and after becoming a way through to understanding the teachings in the *Bhakti-rasamrta-sindhu, bhakti-siddhanta acarya* Sri Sanatana elevated the pure hearts of Rupa's and Jiva's followers and made them drink from the *Bhakti-rasamrta-sindhu.*

Those who have tried to satisfy their own mundane, selfish interests through Jiva Goswami's teachings are bewildered when they read the insurmountable *Durgama-sangamani,* and as a result they are unable to immerse themselves in the *Bhakti-rasamrta-sindhu.* Those who hesitate to call Jiva a *rupanuga*  cannot themselves be identified as *rupanugas* and will thus never be qualified to follow the process of worship taught by Rupa's followers and exemplified by Sri Raghunatha Dasa Goswami. If one does not submit himself to Sri Raghunatha Dasa Goswami Prabhu, Jiva Goswami's followers can offer no alternative. Awakening one’s *sambandha-jnana* allows one’s natural devotional desires to flourish, and the progressive characteristics and variegatedness of devotional service help a devotee strengthen his *sambandha-jnana.*

**The mellows of devotional service; and dry speculative knowledge, cultivating karma, jnana, and yoga, and the unfavorable cultivation of anarthas**

The Supreme Personality of Godhead is the reservoir of transcendental mellows. The pleasure found in objects created by the Lord’s illusory energy is temporary, ignorant, and interrupts the *jivas’* eternal bliss. Being unable to establish their relationship with the Absolute Truth Sri Krsna, conditioned souls covered by gross and subtle designations and full of *anarthas,* become absorbed in the material world, which is simply a transformation of the Lord’s external energy. They fail to understand the difference between Krsna and His energy. In such illusion they call the state devoid of material varigatedness Brahman, and call becoming one with Him *abhidheya.* Some of them, falling into illusion, try to nourish their conditioned life through sense gratification, thinking of themselves as enjoyers of matter.

These two kinds of nondevotional activities, *jnana* and karma, are completely different from the actual nature of devotional service. Nectarean devotional service is never dry, and it has no distasteful properties. If we fail to realize our constitutional position, liberation or material enjoyment will sometimes appear to be the goal of life. Failing to realize the actual nature of devotional service, we may think *bhakti* simply another means to attain liberation or a higher level of material enjoyment. It is by the strength of *bhajana* that pure beings understand that material enjoyment and liberation are not the goals of life. Rather, becoming the master of those two states, these pure beings engage them in the service of supremely opulent *bhakti.*

Fruitive activities (karma) and impersonal knowledge *(jnana*) are subservient to devotional service (*bhakti*). Pure devotional service can never become the maidservant of fruitive activities or mental speculation. The tastelessness of fruitive activities and the dryness of mental speculation are completely opposite in nature to nectarean devotional service. How to favorably cultivate Krsna consciousness and the particular characteristics of devotional service are prominent discussions in the *Bhakti-rasamrta-sindhu.*

In order to support its points, the book also describes the prohibitions and unfavorable moods in relation to practicing devotional service. In the favorable cultivation of Krsna consciousness, the different manifestations of Lord Visnu reveal Krsna's partial opulence. The living entities’ demoniac mood, which is born from restless material intelligence, denies Krsna's supremacy and opulence and clearly reveals the symptoms of a nondevotional character. This is why the living entities who take to cultivating Krsna consciousness in an unfavorable way, who are full of *anarthas* and fallen into illusion, end up in searching for the nonvariegated Brahman \[instead of the personal form of the Lord\]. The degradation caused by the absence of a serving mood - master and servant or worshiped and worshiper - leads people to emphasize the unfavorable cultivation of Krsna consciousness. Incarnations of the kinds of sinful motives promoted by Kamsa, Jar sandha, and so on, manifest in this world and then spread the unfavorable cultivation of Krsna consciousness while considering material enjoyment and liberation the goal of life. That unfavorable cultivation is just another manifestation of the above-mentioned focus on the nonvariegated Brahman.

**The six favorable objects and the covering of pride**

The spiritual master, the pasturing ground, the Vaisnavas, the *brahmanas*, the chanting of the holy names, and the uttering of mantras - these six items can favorably serve the divine young couple of Vraja, or they can be used to create an unfavorable covering over devotional service by becoming opposed to the mood of serving Krsna and maintaining the desire to oneself be the object of service. The six favorable objects for service are helpful for devotional service to the Supreme Lord, but pride can enter and, after covering the truth of these services or items, lead one to use them on the paths of karma, *jnana,* and so on.

**The gradual development of devotional service, which is natural to the soul**

*Sadhana-bhakti* is practiced before one enters a state of *rasa.* In other words, *sadhana-bhakti* is the stage before *rasa.* When *rasa* manifests, *bhakti* is no longer called *sadhana-bhakti* but *bhava-bhakti. Sthayibhava,* or *rati,* is called *sraddha*  in its beginning stage. When four ingredients are mixed with *sthayibhava, rasa* manifests. In the stage of *sadhana,* we find *sraddha,* or firm faith, in spiritual texts.

**Impediments when marching toward the path followed by the rupanugas**

Those unable to appreciate the glories of transcendence tend to consider themselves high-class devotees, even though they are actually less intelligent. Because they do not plunge into the nectarean ocean of *bhakti,* certain of Jiva Goswami's followers forget to identify themselves as *rupanugas* and thus become eternally bereft of surrender to Svarupa's Raghu, who is himself a follower of Rupa. In this way, they commit offenses at the feet of Rupa's followers. Not being able to find any information about devotional service, which is the constitutional propensity of the soul, such conditioned souls sometimes oppose Jiva and sometimes Jiva's follower, Raghunatha. They become bewildered when they learn that the Vaisnavas are guilty of being partial. This very illusion blocks them from following in the footsteps of Rupa. Sometimes they are blocked by the desire for sense gratification, sometimes by a desire to perform fruitive activities that will increase their personal enjoyment in the future, and sometimes by the conclusions of covered Buddhism or Mayavada, which is based on dry renunciation.

**The mellows of devotional service**

The mellows of devotional service are liquid; they cannot be compared to dry, mundane pleasure. There is no shrinking of the eternal ocean of transcendental mellows, and neither does it ever dry up. It is not some small pond. Since the worshipable object is not a commodity of the kingdom of measurement, the nectarean ocean of His service is unlimited - an ocean of spiritual bliss.

**The only way to perform sri-krsna-sankirtana is to submit to Rupa**

If the ambitious conditioned souls, who are afflicted with the disease of materialism, become free from unwanted things and drown in the nectarean ocean of *bhakti,* all types of perfection will be in their reach. The coverings of karma, *jnana,* and so on, and the whimsical desire to misuse one’s life, will not appear in the form of thorns on the path of devotional service.

We can understand that those who have no interest in plunging into the *bhakti-rasamrta-sindhu* due to their previous misdeeds cannot have realized the glories of the mercy of Rupa's followers. In order to exhibit the beauty of the ocean of devotional service, Gaurasundara, who is called “the ocean of mercy” by Damodara Svarupa, accepting the mood of a devotee, arranged for Rupa to sit on the *vyasasana* in order to benefit those who are fond of unfavorable forms of spiritual cultivation. Only Rupa's followers are capable of repeating *Srimad-Bhagavatam.* The illusory mirage presented by the nondevotees can never show the way to the *bhakti-rasamrta-sindhu.* Therefore, surrender to Rupa is the only way to execute *sri-krsna-sankirtana.*

**The sight and teachings of a maha-bhagavata**

The word *para vidya*  refers to devotional service. With a desire to attain that devotional service, today I have come to the lotus feet of the spiritual masters, headed by Sri Vyasa. I ask them to soak my desertlike, nondevotional heart with their mercy and allow me to engage in service by counting me as their eternal servant. As I remember the symptoms of a *maha-bhagavata* as Sri Caitanyadeva described them, I understand that *yāṅhāra darśane mukhe āise kṛṣṇa-nāma tāṅhāre jāniha tumi ‘vaiṣṇava-pradhāna’:* “A first-class Vaisnava is one whose very presence makes others chant the holy name of Krsna.”

Since all of you are only like that, today you have given me the right to worship Sri Vyasa. In exchange for this right, I remain engaged forever in the service of *maha-bhagavatas* like you. I do not wish any other salary.

**Setting the ideal example of a nonduplicitous acarya, which is contrary to the ideal of the pseudo renunciant, who desires material fame, and of offering everything in the service of Hari and guru**

Finally, in conclusion, I request that whatever glorifications you have given me, which only shows your own greatness - I have no qualification to receive them. Therefore, as a servant of the spiritual master I accept those statements as meant for my predecessor  *acaryas* and offer them at their lotus feet. Being driven by the knowledge I have derived from my senses, I have no ability to usurp those statements, because by the order of the Lord I am a weak person who is lower than a blade of grass. Thus I am unable to carry such a heavy burden. I have no other alternative but to pass them on to my spiritual master.

*bhavat mahat samarpitaA na hi*  
*dharttuA prabhav mi vaibhavam*  
*ucitaA gurave ’haA adya tam*  
*suvar ka% praGay t samarpaye*

I am not qualified to accept this offering given by a great personality such as your good self. It is appropriate that today, I, a most fallen person, offer it for my guru out of love.

## Discourse 6

**Aversion and Duplicity**

Champahati Sri Gaura-Gadadhara Temple Courtyard  
Friday, March 2, 1928

**Invocation**

*mukam karoti vachalam*  
*pangum langhayate girim*  
*yat-kripa tam aham vande*  
*shri-gurum dina-taranam*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto the spiritual master by whose mercy a dumb man can become a great speaker and a lame man can cross over a mountain, and who thus delivers all fallen souls.”**

**What need do we have to visit Sri dhama**

Today we are present at Ritudvipa of Sri Navadvipa. Many people may ask what need there is to visit different holy places. If one can sit at home and serve Hari, what need is there to go anywhere?

**Hearing hari-katha and cultivating devotional service in the association of devotees at Sri dhama**

If we sit at home, we cannot meet the devotees. We will not get an opportunity to hear from them. When we have no work, we indulge in sinful activities, including gossiping and blaspheming others, and in these ways, we waste our time. However, if we associate with devotees, we can hear *hari-katha*  and be relieved of the sinful activities we commit due to our misconceptions. If we hear about Lord Hari from the devotees, we can get respite from the troubles we face caused by our fixation on what we learn through our mundane senses.

**Transcendence and the material qualities**

Lord Hari is transcendental. We belong to this world of material qualities. All our senses are arranged so that they can be combined with sense objects. Both are produced by the material modes. That is, our senses can only mingle and reciprocate with objects produced by the three modes of material nature. There is no way to surpass the influence of the three modes of material nature and meet the transcendental object other than by using one’s ears. The function of the six senses is attributed to the objects produced by the three modes. The three material qualities are goodness, passion, and ignorance. Goodness produces worldly auspiciousness; being driven by passion, sometimes we run into auspiciousness and sometimes into inauspiciousness; and in ignorance we rush only toward inauspiciousness. As long as we are alive, our senses are active and useful, but the moment we die they become useless. Then the world produced by the material modes stops before us. But just because the material world stops, the transcendental world does not stop. Rather, it continues to exist and act.

**The material senses are fit to accept sense objects produced by the three modes**

We do not feel the need to acknowledge and appreciate the transcendental world because our senses are useful only for perceiving material objects in the material world. We like to engage in activities that bring us sense enjoyment, and we are tempted by a variety of objects of sense gratification in this world. If needed, we can sometimes even drown in them as we enjoy them, like flies drown in molasses as they try to eat it. We tend to be fond of both the topics we have heard before and those that are new. We are dominated by material form, taste, smell, touch, and sound; these objects engage us in gratifying our senses.

**Sense gratification and material enjoyment**

We want sense gratification. The more someone is able to give me sense pleasure, the dearer that person is to me. We are fond of things that bring immediate pleasure, and thus we live our life in this world. Instead of developing our humanity, our intelligence is gradually drawn downward. We think only of those immediate requirements that keep us materially conditioned. In such conditioned life, the living entities are always averse to the Supreme Lord.

**Transcendental sound vibration appears to benefit the whole world**

The Transcendental Object can appear in this material world out of His own sweet will. He descends into the material world, but being in the material world does not inhibit His transcendental position. Whatever a lump of matter like me speaks is born of material qualities, but whatever we hear through the disciplic succession, that sound has such extraordinary power that when it enters the ears, it can revive our consciousness. The sound vibration that can cross the Viraja River, transcend Brahmaloka, and travel to Vaikuntha, descends into the fourteen worlds and takes us back to the spiritual world. However, the sound vibration produced from the material sky, which remains for some time in the material world and then merges back into the material sky, takes us to hell. These material sounds have been disseminated for our sense gratification and to make us foolish. We can tell a sound is material when its object is to encourage us to eat, sleep, mate, and defend. Such sound conditions us more and more in matter.

Caitanyacandra appeared in a place near here in order to propagate the sound vibration coming from the spiritual sky.

**Association with women and those who associate with women are impediments on the path of hearing spiritual subject matters**

But the merciful topics of the supremely merciful God are not reaching the ears of the people in general. This is because these people remain in the intimate association with women and with those who associate intimately with women. As a result, they remain fully absorbed in thoughts related to women and do not receive mercy.

*Sri Caitanya-candrodaya-namaka* (8.23) states:

*niṣkiñcanasya bhagavad-bhajanonmukhasya*  
*pāraṁ paraṁ jigamiṣor bhava-sāgarasya*  
*sandarśanaṁ viṣayiṇām atha yoṣitāṁ ca*  
*hā hanta hanta viṣa-bhakṣaṇato ’py asādhu*

**“Alas, for a person who is seriously desiring to cross the material ocean and engage in the transcendental loving service of the Lord without material motives, seeing a materialist engaged in sense gratification or seeing a woman who is similarly interested is more abominable than drinking poison willingly.”**

Women and those who want to enjoy them appeared at the beginning of creation. Their descendants have flooded the world with people who associate with women for sense gratification. This has created havoc in the world. However, the devotees of Mahaprabhu are not fond of such association. *Sri Caitanya-caritamrta (Antya* 6.220) states:

*mahāprabhura bhakta-gaṇera vairāgya pradhāna*  
*yāhā dekhi’ prīta hana gaura-bhagavān*

**“Renunciation is the basic principle sustaining the lives of Sri Caitanya Mahaprabhu's devotees. Seeing this renunciation, Sri Caitanya Mahaprabhu, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, is extremely satisfied.”**

**Who is qualified to hear hari-katha?**

To think that Mahaprabhu is my personal gardener and that He will supply me bouquets of flowers is the mood of an enjoyer rather than a servant. The Supreme Lord is the controller of everything. Mahaprabhu has exposed the faulty conclusions and opposing mentalities of those who maintain the sinful desire to enjoy Maya, attempting to show how expert they are in remaining attentive to material sound. Only the fortunate can understand what I am talking about. The unfortunate may think they are hearing, but they are not; they are deceived.

If out of good fortune we become interested in serving the Worshipable Object, only then will we be able to hear and understand spiritual topics. One has to rise above his present condition. Yamaraja will not spare those who smear stool all over their bodies just to avoid their smell. At every moment the illusory energy leads us toward the kingdom of aversion to God. The moment we have no protector, everything around us will turn into an enemy and attack us. The moment we refuse to hear *hari-katha* from a genuine *sadhu,* we will have failed to serve that *sadhu* with our sincerity. Then Maya will take advantage and devour us.

**The pretense of hari-katha and kirtana by so-called learned and professional reciters**

Those who have lost their spiritual propensity by choosing to think that satisfying their animal propensities is the goal of human life cannot hear about Lord Hari. Therefore, our duty is to seek out *hari-katha,* spiritual subject matters. There are millions of people in the world who are expert in exercising their brain and tongue with Sanskrit grammar but who cannot realize the purport of the transcendental or spiritual sound vibration that has appeared from the spiritual sky. These people cannot speak *hari-katha.* Their words are just like gramophone songs. Such people drown themselves in sense gratification and never realize the truth.

Material objects produce contrary results: learning brings foolishness, happiness brings distress, and distress brings happiness. Those who are intelligent should think how to benefit themselves and never become bereft of mercy.

**Why does one choose to live sinfully?**

A person may have once had good intentions, but after some time he again turns to sinful life. Why? It is because he did not spare time to hear the transcendental topics of Hari, or perhaps he became inattentive, only pretending to hear. He did not at all try to stay aloof from his endeavors for apparent happiness; rather, he became busy looking for sensory happiness on the advice of crooked people. If we take shelter of the Lord’s lotus feet, whether we are educated or uneducated, whether we have strength or no strength, there will be no problem. Actually, the living entity is transcendental. It is only when he thinks himself bound by the qualities of material nature that he becomes attached to the material world.

**The devotees of the Lord are the most benevolent persons**

The servants of the Supreme Lord appear in this world for the welfare of human beings. They have no material duty to fulfill. The only duty these most merciful personalities have is to divert the adverse, material tastes of the living entities toward Krsna. To give food to the hungry and other such acts of mundane charity are useless wastes of labor if we forget the real goal of life.

**Every object of sense gratification is Maya’s bait**

The living entities are captivated at every moment within the kingdom of sense enjoyment. Maya constantly captures us by offering us different types of bait. Just as a wild male elephant is captured by tempting it with a she elephant, so the illusory energy, Maya devi, traps us in the world of birth and death by tempting us with women and other forms of sense enjoyment. Thinking the illusory objects real, the living entities run after them. Just to deceive human beings, Maya keeps different types of happiness on her hook. Whatever appears good and beautiful when seen with the eyes of material enjoyment is just another of Maya’s hooks. Those who indulge in sense gratification will be hooked. Eat, sleep, and be merry – and then go to hell: this thinking has ruined human society. What can be more shameful than this!

**The tactic for protecting the sense enjoyers**

Sri Gaurasundara made arrangements to protect human beings from such thinking, just as a doctor administers quinine with a sugar coating.
