The material sound from the material sky constantly allures human beings to grasp material objects. It is this sound that is causing all the problems. Being attracted by it, human beings are shot with Maya’s arrows, just as a deceptive hunter lures a deer and then shoots it. This is why Gaurasundara arranged for *hari-katha*  accompanied by musical instruments and melody. This is like coating the bitter quinine with gelatin. Normally, dancing, singing, and playing musical instruments lead people to commit sin, and are therefore engaged in by only the most sinful people. If these people are not engaged in the service of K madeva Krsna, they will surely have to vomit poison in the end.

**Duplicity is praised in a world filled with duplicity**

Devotees, who do not mislead the living entities, are not respected in society. At present, there are those who misguide people in the name of speaking *hari-katha,* and to be cheated by such persons has become standard in this age. Real devotees, those who want to expose the nondevotees, are often called “thieves” and “nondevotees” by these same thieves and cheaters. Maya will not allow people to become sincere. Rather, she invents so many ways and means to keep people away from the association of real devotees.

**Mundane sahajiyas and their illegal, envious, sense gratificatory endeavors**

*Rasa-lila*  performances are going on at Kuliya – so many listeners! The *kirtana* performers show their skills! But as one goes to hell by gaining only a material education, so also are people going to hell by hearing songs about Radha and Krsna. This is because these songs are not being sung to gratify the senses of the only enjoyer, Sri Kamadeva, or Krsna; these singers are themselves busy posing as Kamadeva. Such people cannot sing *bhajanas* about Radha and Krsna. Just as worms suck people’s blood and do not allow them to become nourished by the food they eat, so people’s attempts at spirituality can be different ways to lead them to inauspiciousness. How can those who have not conquered their senses sing or hear songs of Rai-Kanu, or Radha-Krsna?

How an arrangement meant for Mahadeva be taken by an insignificant person like me? So many people rush to drink deadly poison – they accept pots of poison as nectar! Will the loud shout of the  *acarya* not enter their ears even once? An experienced physician tries his best to cure the disease of the afflicted without charging a consultation fee, and yet the sick try their best to be rid of that physician! They are digging their own graves. They are cutting off the branch on which they are sitting.

**Duplicity and weakness; a cheater is never benefited**

Duplicity is one thing and weakness is another. One who is nonduplicitous achieves his fortune. “I will cheat my spiritual master,” “I will lie to my physician,” – I nourish the black cobra of my sinful propensity by feeding it with milk and bananas, which I hide in the hole of duplicity. I will not let people know about my sins. Instead, I will collect from people name and fame as a great devotee.” These mentalities are not just weakness of the heart but a dangerous type of duplicity. People who suffer from it will never find benefit. Those who have obstructed their path to auspiciousness will not achieve auspiciousness. One gradually achieves auspiciousness in life by sincerely associating with devotees and submissively hearing spiritual discussions from their mouths. If we make only a show of *sadhu-sanga,* then our hellish mentality will increase day by day.

In the example set by Gaurasundara there is no room for duplicity. Chota Haridasa was duplicitous. If someone like me puts on the garb of a devotee but behaves as a nondevotee, if after accepting the renounced order someone like me maintains the sinful mentality Ravana had when he kidnapped Sita, then he is slitting his own throat. That is, he is doing something else in the guise of worshiping Hari!

If we are weak at heart from our millions of births in this world, there is no harm, but if we take shelter of duplicity even once – if we try to kidnap Sita while disguised as a devotee – then we wrap the snake of inconvenience around our neck forever. It is better to live as an animal, bird, insect, fly, or a member of any of the millions of species than to take shelter of duplicity.

Lord Gaurasundara never shows mercy to a cheater. *Srimad-Bhagavatam* (2.7.42) states:

*yeṣāṁ sa eṣa bhagavān dayayed anantaḥ*  
*sarvātmanāśrita-pado yadi nirvyalīkam*  
*te dustarām atitaranti ca deva-māyāṁ*  
*naiṣāṁ mamāham iti dhīḥ śva-śṛgāla-bhakṣye*

**“But anyone who is specifically favored by the Supreme Lord, the Personality of Godhead, due to unalloyed surrender unto the service of the Lord, can overcome the insurmountable ocean of illusion and can understand the Lord. But those who are attached to this body, which is meant to be eaten at the end by dogs and jackals, cannot do so.”**

**What is the cause of degradation?**

The moment we forget “who am I?” our degradation begins. We become absorbed in various temptations extended to us by material existence. The moment we become even slightly careless, the witch of the illusory energy immediately captures us by the neck and devours us. Unless we constantly hear the topics of the Supreme Lord meant for swanlike persons, there is no way to free ourselves of the clutch of the illusory energy. *Srimad-Bhagavatam* (6.3.28) confirms this:

*tān ānayadhvam asato vimukhān mukunda-*  
*pādāravinda-makaranda-rasād ajasram*  
*niṣkiñcanaiḥ paramahaṁsa-kulair asaṅgair*  
*juṣṭād gṛhe niraya-vartmani baddha-tṛṣṇān*

***“Paramahamsas*** **are exalted persons who have no taste for material enjoyment and who drink the honey of the Lord’s lotus feet. My dear servants, bring to me for punishment only persons who are averse to the taste of that honey, who do not associate with** ***paramahamsas,*** **and who are attached to family life and worldly enjoyment, which form the path to hell.”**

**Simplicity is brahmanism, which is subordinate to Vaisnavism**

All of you please bless me so that the witch of duplicity may not devour me. The Vaisnavas who want our benefit say that another name for VaicGavism is “simplicity.” The servants of the swanlike Vaisnavas are simple-minded, and that is why they are the best of the *brahmanas.*

*rjjavaA brahmaGe s ks t \[udre ’n rjjva-lakcaGam”*

**“A** ***brahmana*** **is honest or simple, and a** ***sudra*** **is just the opposite.”**

**Teaching people by humbling oneself: how a nonenvious acarya, distressed to see the distress of others, teaches**

I am not saying this with an oblique hint to anyone but with utmost honesty: I pray to all of you that you kindly bless me so that I can sincerely serve the transcendental Personality of Godhead. My condition is pathetic. No one is more fallen than me. All of you kindly protect me. This is my appeal at your feet. Please uplift me. If you help me make advancement, then you will be greatly benefited. Anyone who protects me – the Supreme Lord will definitely protect him. I do not know about Lord Hari, but I have some curiosity about Him. Still, at every step, pious activities, sinful activities, welfare activities, and so on teach me duplicity by pushing me toward material sense gratification. All of you kindly help me so that I can achieve auspiciousness in life. This is my request of all of you.

## Discourse 7

**The Object of Sri Gaudia Matha's Preaching**

Place: Nirshachati, Manbhum, Purulia, at the house of Bhakti Bhusana Indran arayanacandra Mahasaya on March 25, 1928

**What the Gaudia Matha appears to preach**

As Pradyunma Misra made a mistake when seeing the characteristics of Raya Ramananda, many people make mistakes when looking at the preaching of Sri Gaudiya Matha. This is due to their foolishness. Because in the name of “great religious preachers” or “great philanthropists” some people have ruined the brains of the younger generation in Bengal, we have to waste hundreds of gallons of blood, and still only a few people are able to grasp the truth. It is always a fact that there are not many customers for truth, because truth is not palatable, even though it is so beneficial.

**People subscribe to immediate enjoyment by the force of popular opinion; customers who want ultimate benefit are extremely rare**

The *Katha Upanisad* (1.2.2) says:

*śreyaś ca preyaś ca manuṣyam etas tau*  
*samparītya vivinakti dhīraḥ*  
*śreyo hi dhīro'bhipreyaso vṛṇīte*  
*preyo mando yoga kṣemād vṛṇīte*

***“Sreyas*** **and** ***preyas*** **are inseparably connected to human beings, but sober persons, realizing the meaning of both, determine that** ***sreyas*** **is the cost of liberation and while focus only on** ***preyas,*** **causes bondage. Those seeking liberation give up** ***preyas*** **and accept** ***sreyas,*** **whereas foolish people pray for** ***preyas*** **in the form of achieving things that they lack and preserving those things they already have.”**

Also from the same *Katha Upanisad* verse (1.2.7):

*śravaṇayāpi bahubhir yo na labhyaḥ*  
*śṛṇvanto 'pi bahavo yaṁ na vidyuḥ*  
*āścaryo vaktā kuśalo 'sya labdhā*  
*āścaryo 'sya jñātā kuśalānuśiṣṭaḥ*

**People of the world know everything!**

The people of the world are drowning in an ocean of nescience, yet they think themselves very smart and all-knowing. Being covered by illusion, they wander about, up and down, in the material universe. Being led by such blind people, all the other blind people in the world are falling into a ditch.

The *Katha Upanisad* (1.2.5) states:

*avidyāyām antare vartamānāḥ*  
*svayaṁ dhīrāḥ paṇḍitam-manyamānāḥ*  
*jaṅghanyamānāḥ pariyanti mūḍhā*  
*andhenaiva nīyamānā yathāndhāḥ*

**“Caught in the grip of ignorance, self-proclaimed experts consider themselves learned authorities. They wander about this world befooled, like the blind leading the blind.”**

**The masses are not able to accept the difference between proper renunciation and pseudo renunciation, and so misunderstand the value of proper renunciation**

Rupa Goswami Prabhu gave the Gaudiya Vaisnavas two mottos:

*prāpañcikatayā buddhyā hari-sambandhi-vastunaḥ*  
*mumukṣubhiḥ parityāgo vairāgyaṁ phalgu kathyate*

**“One who rejects anything without knowledge of its relationship to Krsna is incomplete in his renunciation.” (*****Bhakti-rasamrta-sindhu*** **1.2.256)**

And:

*anāsaktasya viṣayān yathārham upayuñjataḥ*  
*nirbandhaḥ kṛṣṇa-sambandhe yuktaṁ vairāgyam ucyate*

**“Things should be accepted for the Lord’s service and not for one’s personal sense gratification. If one accepts something without attachment and accepts it because it is related to Krsna, one’s renunciation is called** ***yukta-vairagya.”***

Even Sanskrit scholars do not understand the actual meaning of these two verses. This is true even if these verses are translated into Bengali for these scholars - they simply cannot understand what Rupa Goswami is teaching. The day they understand the meaning of these two verses will be the day they realize that whatever they have understood as dharma is really “renunciation” and “austerity”; whatever their endeavors, whatever the expertise they exhibited to the world - everything has been wrong and, as such, been a useless waste of their time.

**Bold impartial truth**

Millions of speakers who do not speak impartial truth will go to hell; but the impartial truth that is being spoken boldly, even after hundreds of lifetimes and hundreds of ***yugas****,* will somehow or other come to be recognized as confidential truth and its great importance realized. Until one spends millions of gallons of hard-earned blood, it is not possible to make a person understand the actual truth. It is not easy for a teacher to teach what it means to be a devotee.

**Business in the name of dharma, and the activities of Sri Gaudiya Matha**

The religious principles preached by Srinivasa, Narottama, and Syamananda have been terribly distorted by the society of sense enjoyers! Today, the teachings of Sri Caitanyadeva, the Six Goswamis, and the three  *acaryas* headed by Srinivasa have been submerged in the depths of the ocean. The act of being  *acarya* has now become a business; the spiritual master “serves” his disciples only in the name of “guru” and gives mantra initiation to prostitutes. Every rich materialist has prostitutes, and such people hire so-called gurus to give mantras to their prostitutes. These so-called gurus do not hesitate to indulge in such illegal, unrighteous activities, knowing that if they didn’t, their business would be hampered. They cannot refuse the requests of the rich, because if they did, the rich would reject them. Some of these gurus simply sit in a solitary place, sing and play musical instruments, and thereby increase their bile. There is no benefit in such a rat race or rat *kirtana.*

To state this in different language, the endeavors of so-called gurus is not dharma but brokerage for or the temptation to perform wickedness. What selfish people do in the name of compassion, to be frank, is just that - in the *name* of compassion - and nothing else. As a fish dies because of its attraction for the hook, and an animal dies because it is lured by the hunter’s music, so mankind is gliding down to hell with its ambition to fulfill the promise of sense pleasure. To check their progress in their illegal activities is one of the major duties of the Gaudiya Matha.

**The unique example of feeling distressed by seeing others’ distress**

We are ready to spend two hundred gallons of blood to save one person - if even a single person is interested in hearing the truth. Let each and every selfless, merciful member of the Gaudiya Matha be prepared to spend two hundred gallons of blood for nourishing the spiritual body of each and every person in human society. Millions of wicked people are approaching innocent, rich people, who do not know what is good and what is bad, and taking them to hell. The Gaudiya Matha never indulges in or encourages such envious activities.

**Truth is not irrelevant**

We often think discussions of reality are irrelevant because we cannot grasp them. Just because I am inattentive, an opportunist, or a sense enjoyer, that does not make the truth spoken by the  *acarya* irrelevant.

**Sri Gaudiya Matha does not preach mental speculation**

The preachers of the Gaudiya Matha are not mental speculators. They are not driven by imagination. This wicked mind, this stupid mind, is very fond of serving lust, anger, and other such vices. The wicked mind sometimes puts on the dress of an instructor in order to employ the whole world in the service of lust, anger, and so on.

**The characteristics of real welfare**

Being averse to Lord Visnu from top to bottom, millions of living entities have arrived in Mahamaya's prison house to envy the Supreme Lord in millions of ways. If you can save one person from this prison house, then it will be more benevolent than opening millions of hospitals. The real mercy, which does not produce any ill effect, does not last only a few days; it is not meant to mitigate hunger for a day or two. The real and ultimate mercy was bestowed by Sri Caitanyadeva, and it is eternal.

**A well-wisher who encourages sense gratification is not an acarya**

Imagine I am suffering from indigestion, so I call a doctor and tell him, “Prescribe fried rice and fish curry for my diet.” The doctor then arranges tilings according to my taste and enjoying spirit and leaves after collecting his fee. Such a person cannot be called a doctor. A guru or real preacher is not a flatterer. To become popular and to run their business, there are those who keep pace with the taste and enjoying spirit of millions of diseased persons. These cheaters are not the real well-wishers of the diseased. Rather, they are the opposite of well wishers. We should never listen to such people. If I call a doctor to treat my sickness and then dictate to him my treatment, then it was useless to have called the doctor. It is like digging one’s own grave.

Genuine doctors do not prescribe according to the patient’s dictation, whereas those who are clever, cheating, so-called doctors, whose aim is simply to collect fees, look to burden their pockets rather than the future cure of the patient. Such persons are not acaryas. *Acaryas* are not gurus whom I can reject any time they say things not to my liking or who simply approve my wicked propensities. If a four-year-old child wants to understand the conjugal mellows, or a seven-year-old boy wants to understand Shakespeare’s poetry, how will it be possible? We won’t benefit by hearing their words. By misusing their independence, the living entities can dig their own grave or kill their own goats by chopping them in the rump instead of cutting their throats.

**Philosophies other than the philosophy of Sri Caitanya are unautheorized**

I am a resident of India, so it is my duty to see to the interests of India at least from the temporary point of view. On the other hand, if I take birth in a foreign country, then although it is against India’s interests, my duty would be to see the foreign interest. Sri Caitanya and the His self-realized devotees are not so narrow-minded or sectarian. They do not preach based on material time, place, or candidate. The welfare work they are performing, the ideal of patriotism they display, is not for the temporary benefit of one person while it harms another person. This welfare work serves the whole country, the whole world, the entire population, for all time. This is not a fable; this is the biggest truth.

**Experimental knowledge and its ultimate limit, impersonalism**

On the bank of a vast river sat a few smokers smoking cigars. When they felt the need to relight the cigar, one of them, sitting on this side of the river, stretched his hand toward a lamp burning in a boat on the other side of the river. Seeing that the cigar was not lighting, another smoker snatched it and stretched his hand a little further toward the lamp. Such is the endeavor of those who reach for experimental knowledge! The river is one-mile-wide, and yet sitting on this side these smokers dare to try to light their cigar from the lamp of the other side on the river! They dare to try to touch the light on the other side of the Viraj River with their mundane education and intelligence! Another empiricist comes and tells them to stretch their hand of experience a little further. But the hand of material experience cannot touch the illumination of Vaikuntha. The hand of worldly experience cannot be stretched so far. That is why these people often become so tired that they end up becoming impersonalists. They are tired of trying to expand, and finally heave a sigh of relief, saying ‘To infinity.”

**The ignorant fruitive workers**

There is no one more foolish in this world than the one who engages in perishable material activities. Those who run behind such persons, thinking them leaders, will never find water in the mirage those so-called leaders create. How long can people beg for the proposed favor of fruitive workers? Who can even get it? Where can one get it? Not thinking about this, almost one hundred percent of the population is rushing along on the path of illusion! Those who have developed even a slight service attitude toward the dust of Sri Caitanya’s lotus feet are able to consider the positions of demigods like Brahma , B\[haspati, and India insignificant – to reject those positions as stool and urine. To stop one’s focus on material enjoyment and liberation is called *bhakti.* Sri Caitanya’s servants do not beg for material enjoyment or liberation; they are not cheaters.

**The living entities’ self-deception and the act of utmost compassion**

Alas! Nowadays, Maya dev+’s servants are being counted as servants of Sri Caitanya. If we accept them as “devotees,” then there is no one more foolish than us. 99.9 percent of the world’s population are chopping with an ax at Caitanyacandra’s lotus feet in the name of offering Him flowers. Almost cent percent of the people are doing that. To remove their illusion is an act of the greatest compassion. Doing so is the true path of liberation, not a way to get material enjoyment. We cannot serve these people by flattering them; we cannot give a certificate of learning to fools. In no way does Caitanyadeva act to approve the foolishness of sense gratification.

**The religion propagated by Sri Caitanyadeva, and Christianity**

While traveling from Mirikh to Naimisharanya, I had some discussion about Christianity with a Reverend Stanley Jones. I informed him that Mr. Kennedy has distorted Sri Caitanyadeva’s religion in his book *The Chaitanya Movement.* Mr. Kennedy thinks Sri Caitanyadeva’s religion is less ethical than Christianity. I told Reverend Stanley Jones that the presently propagated Christianity can also advance in moral standards if Christian preachers impartially discuss the characteristics of Caitanya with the genuine servants of Caitanya.

**Concocted Gauranga-isms, or idolatry**

Not a single word of Caitanyadeva is entering the ears of human beings. They have concocted Caitanyadeva in their own whimsical manner and thus made the absolute Caitanya, who is one without a second, “my Gauranga,” “your Gauranga,” “the Gauranga of those who worship ghosts and witches,” “the Gauranga of the sense gratifiers,” “the Gauranga of ula-Baula-Kartabhaja-Kisoribhaja-Ne a-Ne i-Sakhibhekhi-Navarasika,” “the Gauranga of the mundane *sahajiyas,”* and “the Gauranga of the Nagaris.” There is also the Any bhilasi’s Gauranga, the Gauranga of the *karmis, jnanis,* and yogis, the *smartas'* Gauranga and so many others. All these are forms of idolatry, concocted by particular classes of people.

**Sri Caitanya is not within the grasp of a mental speculator’s imagination**

The transcendental *saccidananda* form of Sri Krsna seen within the pure heart by devotees whose eyes are anointed with the ointment of love is Krsna's actual form. We reject whatever forms of Krsna are painted through the use of the mundane imagination by people who desire to gratify their senses - for example, Ravi Verma’s *Krishna,* the Calcutta Art School’s *Krishna,* Bengal’s *Krishna,* Bombay’s *Krishna,* the Krishna that was painted in Germany... all are imaginary idols. Similarly, “my Gauranga,” “your Gauranga,” and the Gaur Egas of the *smartas, sahajiyas,* and Nagari’s are also idols, all *may ,* all matter. Gauranga is not an “idol.” He is the supremely cognizant Supreme Personality of Godhead. Since He is not an idol concocted by conditioned souls, He is Sri Krsna Caitanya. He is never “created” by any conditioned soul in this world. The conditioned souls cannot manufacture Sri Caitanya in their factories of mental speculation by molding Him in matter.

**The perverted image of Sri Caitanya painted by the godless society; fresh opinions have become unnecessary since Sri Caitanya’s appearance**

Some people have painted Caitanyadeva in such a way that we feel embarrassed to call ourselves His followers. We are so unfortunate that even after Sri Caitanyadeva’s appearance, various opinions averse to His teachings have been preached in our country. Since we have no interest in hearing the eternal topics describing Sri Caitanyadeva, we have created new philosophies in our country. In order to make everyone spiritually inclined, Sri Caitanya engaged Haridasa and Nityananda to preach door to door; We are Hari’s eternal servants; it is the eternal, spontaneous propensity of the spirit soul to serve Hari; only service to Hari awards eternal happiness and frees us of our thirst for temporary, incomplete, material pleasures; only service to Hari allows us to regain our full independence. But being indifferent to Caitanyadeva’s teachings, we continue to search for pieces of glass outside while ignoring the invaluable treasury we have at home.

**The descriptions of devotional service are not fairy tales concocted by fruitive workers or the Gaudiya magazine or Gaudiya Matha**

Daring to criticize the activities of the Lord’s devotees with our fruitive mentality is like a penny of a frog. We think that descriptions of devotional service are like the fairy tales our grandmother used to tell us - “O moon! Please come down to earth and play with my loving child.” The *Gaudiya* and the Gaudiya Matha are built by many sincere and accomplished persons expending many gallons of blood to serve the world. From the external point of view, this blood is sucked out from others and then fully dedicated to the service of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Still, let people hear attentively, understand Sri Caitanya’s teachings, and bring about people’s real benefit.

**Godless human society and Sri Caitanya’s followers**

Generally, people say, “If you can waste your time on the topics of direct sense perception, if you can supply fuel to such topics, and if you can surrender to the dictates of your patients, we will call you *‘Sadhu.’’’*

But we reject, like stool and urine, the fame that comes from being such a *sadhu.* Instead, we propose to follow the path of real *sadhus,* who are followers of the lotus feet of Caitanyadeva.

**Sri Gaudiya Matha is not a theater for staging the drama of duplicity**

When the Gaudiya Matha started its preaching, a few lawyers in Howrah suggested we join with such-and-such mission. We replied, “Our path is completely opposite to the proposed path of thousands of such missions.” To this they said, “That means your path is unattractive, for there is no compassion in your philosophy.” I replied, “Only by our teachings will real mercy be exhibited; the mercy the world proposes - that apparently pleasant form of compassion - is covered envy in the name of compassion. I am prepared to prove the truth of what I’m saying, so let anyone come forward and refute me.”

Then some of them said, “The best example of Mahaprabhu’s mercy is to open a maternity home.” Following the example of maternity homes in Western countries, people wearing flower garlands, in order to make a show, fill their pockets and then commit adultery behind the curtain. These people open up such “factories” and thereby create a barrier for people who had hoped to realize the real nature of Caitanyadeva’s compassion. Sri Caitanya is an ocean of mercy, and His mercy produces no harm to any being. Opening maternity homes and other such activities may buy popularity, but such self-deception and cheating of others is many *yojanas* away from Mah prabhu’s nonduplicitous dharma of compassion.

To protect the delivery of unchaste women - to encourage immorality in the name of morality – has often put forward to exemplify Mah prabhu’s mercy. The Gaudiya Matha says that hypocrites who encourage immorality should be awarded more punishment than the Indian penal court can presently award. Mahaprabhu taught this in His pastime of punishing Chota Haridasa. Haridasa, on the order of Bhagavan acarya, begged for some rice from Madhavi Mat in the name of Mahaprabhu’s service. The Supreme Judge, the creator, awarded Haridasa a death sentence. Caitanyadeva’s doors are closed to those who have the inclination to kidnap another’s wife, especially if such men kidnap women while they are in the renounced order. Caitanyadeva and His servants do not look at the faces of such persons. The punishment of such persons is to drown in the river. *Prakrti darsana kaile ei prayascitta:* “If with sensual intentions one looks at women, this is the only process of atonement.”

**Sentimental songs and Sri Gaudiya Matha**

Sri Krsna displays His pastimes in this world in order to destroy the freedom to be hypocritical that materialists adopt. The Radha-Krsna *bhajanas* that are being passed off as written by Srinivasa and Syamananda Prabhu – Sri Gaudiya Matha is deadly against those songs. Rather, the Matha wants to nourish people’s spiritual lives by presenting pure songs about Radha-Krsna. This is the aim of the Gaudiya Matha.

Sri Gaudiya Matha will never allow conditioned souls greedy for money, women, and fame to drink the deadly poison fit only for Sad Siva, who is free from the clutches of material nature. Some may see us as merciless, but the Gaudiya Matha is not in favor of envying conditioned souls by deceiving them and serving their tastes. Tolerating the criticism of its patients and becoming unpopular among those the Gaudiya Matha intends to serve, Sri Gaudiya Matha thinks only of the patients’ benefit. What a great sacrifice, including the giving up of fame! What a great tendency to do good to others! But those who are deceived by materialistic *sadhus* will not understand it.

**The history of revolution in the kingdom of spirituality**

Let the spiritualists contemplate how many great revolutions have occurred in the history of spirituality as a result of the Gaudiya Mamha’s preaching. Each and every member of the Gaudiya Matha has dedicated his life. They are preaching to people something brand new. What a revolution! The members of the Gaudiya Matha are not afraid of the millions of so-called learned persons of the world. They are not willing to encourage duplicity in debauchees. Innumerable godless people are inventing different sinful policies in order to steal the revenue of the undisputed, transcendental King of kings, Sri Visvambhara. The Gaudiya Matha is ready to sacrifice such a mentality in the sacrificial fire. They do not want a *paisa* from anyone. Rather, they want to give to the world the fully cognizant spiritual object, Caitanyadeva. They say, “Whatever wealth or property you have, offer everything at the lotus feet of Krsna, who is the controller of all.” To those who are prepared to give everything to the lotus feet of the Supreme Lord, the Gaudiya Matha is prepared to provide them full information about the Lord’s lotus feet.

**The purpose of the occasional festivals held by Gaudiya Matha**

The Gaudiya Matha is not a place to rendezvous for eating and sleeping. To increase the quantity of stool and urine by eating, sleeping, or taking intoxicants – these are not the purposes of the Gaudiya Matha. The Gaudiya Matha holds festivals in order to give nondevotees fond of pompous functions an opportunity to hear *hari-katha .*

**The hidden desire to prove God formless**

To enhance our abominable debauchery and to negate the idea of intense love for Krsna, we like to say that God is formless, that He has no eternal form. If we can prove that God is devoid of hands and legs, then we can nicely plunder and exploit the world with our own hands and legs! If God doesn’t have a form, doesn’t have eyes, we can secretly commit adultery. After all, God cannot see us. We think wealth, women, and fame - for that matter, the whole world - is meant for our enjoyment and not for God’s enjoyment. This is why our hidden agenda is to make God impersonal. Other than a pure devotee, everyone, including the *karmis, jnanis,* yogis, and ascetics, want to prove God impersonal. All the mental speculators of the world want to make God impersonal. They think, “We will enjoy and be glorified. Why should God get everything?”

**The spiritual harmony of material and spiritual morality in the supremely independent God**

Following the injunction of the Vedas, the Gaudiya Matha says, “God alone will enjoy everything.” God alone should be intensely attached. Krsna's activities cannot be measured by the standards of worldly morality. If Krsna alone is the proprietor of all material and spiritual wealth, then He is free to enjoy His own property. There is nothing unethical or objectionable or debauched about it. What is debauchery in one philosophy or language may be the highest morality in another. What is debauched and extremely abominable and disgusting for living entities to do in this world is supremely relishable in the faultless spiritual abode, and it enhances the delight of the eternal mellows.

**The only difference between a materialist and a renunciant is that the former enjoys his senses openly and the latter enjoys his senses covertly**

The self-deceptive, greedy materialists and renunciants think, “We will go to hell, but we will enjoy, ride horses or be carried on palanquins, live in nice buildings, watch attractive forms, smell sweet aromas, relish the four types of delicious foods, hear sweet voices, and touch soft things! The renunciants, because they have not been able to enjoy these things for a long time, put on the dress of a pseudo renunciant and become angry at their sense objects. They are just like a henpecked husband who quarrels with his wife. Such renunciants are dissatisfied, attached, angry cheaters.

The Gaudiya Matha does not propose or encourage such renunciation or material enjoyment. Rather, the Gaudiya Matha says that Krsna alone is the undisputed enjoyer. Krsna alone will ride on a horse or palanquin. Krsna alone will live in the nice building. All forms are meant for the satisfaction of Krsna's eyes alone, all eatables for His palate, all soft objects for the festival of His touch. Those who have become mini Krsnas by forgetting the service of He who is the supreme enjoyer are pierced by Maya devi after being tempted by her various lures in the form of taste, smell, touch, sight, and sound.

**Renunciation in the Gaudiya Matha and how most people understand renunciation**

The renunciation practiced by the devotees of Mahaprabhu, by members of the Gaudiya Matha, is not false renunciation. Some people say, “I have given up my ten-cubit-long *dhoti* and am now wearing only a five-cubit-long *dhoti.”* Another says, “I have given up wearing shoes.” Someone else says, “I have stopped eating.” These renunciants may purchase praise from materialistic people by their behaviors, but to Mah prabhu’s devotees, their duplicity is exposed.

**The characteristics of the servants of the Gaudiya Matha**

The life and wealth of each member of the Gaudiya Matha is helping him to realize the Supreme Personality of Godhead. As the members make advancement in spiritual life, they help others accordingly. No one in the Gaudiya Matha collects even a *paisa* with which to maintain himself or to fuel the unrestricted sense gratification of his so-called mortal relatives the way stipend-holders or so-called spiritual masters do. Every last penny collected by the servants of the Gaudiya Matha, which they receive after working hard from morning to evening, is spent to remove the illusion of the people of this world. The members of the Gaudiya Matha cannot eat many sweets. They cannot enjoy snuff, tea, betel nuts, eggs, crab, flesh and blood, tobacco, cigars, and they don’t wear silk saffron cloth. Instead, they eat the cheap rice Lord Visvambhara gave them as His *prasada.* They are glad to honor such delicious *prasada* and remain engaged in His service morning to night.

**Sri Gaudiya Matha is the flag-bearer for Sri Gaura and His devotees**

It is not that Caitanyacandra came 440 years ago and is now dead. He is eternally present, and He has engaged the Gaudiya Matha in His mission. Sri Gaurahari does not allow the most experienced and intelligent to inflict worldly distress on the members of the Matha the way that punishable conditioned souls suffer. Therefore, remembering that the Vaisnavas are worshipable, the servants of the Matha servants serve them. Neither does He allow the foolish people to become envious.

The devotee is called the asraya, and the devotee’s beloved, Krsna, is the visaya.

Henotheism is the belief in and worship of a single god while accepting the existence or possible existence of other deities that may also be served. Max Muller brought the term into wider usage in his discussions of Hinduism and the Rg Veda. Henotheists may conceptualize different aspects of the one deity by worshiping several deities, such as in five-god worship.

Scholar Melville Kennedy, who wrote The Religious Life of India, the Chaitanya Movement: A Study of the Vaishnavism of Bengal, *published in 1925.*

Violet, indigo, blue, green, yellow, orange, and red - the colors of the rainbow, in order.

Buddhists have a system of deity worship they use to aid their impersonal meditation. It is not devotional sendee.

Panentheism considers God and the world to be interrelated, with the world being in God and God being in the world. Panentheism seeks to avoid either isolating God from the world as traditional theism often does or identifying God with the world as pantheism does. Traditional theistic systems emphasize the difference between God and the world while panentheism stresses God’s active presence in the world. Pantheism emphasizes God’s presence in the world, but panentheism maintains the identity and significance of the nondivine.

The *Durgama-sangamani* is Srila Jiva Goswami's commentary on the *Bhakti-rasamrta-sindhu.*
